## 新投稿 (139)

<h3 id="1">[1] Understanding Annotator Safety Policy with Interpretability</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.05329](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05329)
- **Authors**: Alex Oesterling, Donghao Ren, Yannick Assogba, Dominik Moritz, Sunnie S.Y. Kim, Leon Gatys, Fred Hohman
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Interpretability, AI Safety
- **Venue**: ACM FAccT 2026
- **Summary**: 本文提出了注释者策略模型(APMs)，这是一种可解释模型，仅通过标注行为就能学习注释者的内部安全策略，无需额外标注工作。APMs能够揭示策略歧义和价值多元性，支持更具针对性、透明性和包容性的安全策略设计。

<h3 id="2">[2] ZAYA1-8B Technical Report</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.05365](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05365)
- **Authors**: Robert Washbourne, Rishi Iyer, Tomas Figliolia, Henry Zheng, Ryan Lorig-Roach, Sungyeon Yang, Pritish Yuvraj, Quentin Anthony, Yury Tokpanov, Xiao Yang, Ganesh Nanduru, Stephen Ebert, Praneeth Medepalli, Skyler Szot, Srivatsan Rajagopal, Alex Ong, Bhavana Mehta, Beren Millidge
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Mixture-of-Experts, Mathematical Reasoning
- **Summary**: 本文介绍了ZAYA1-8B，一个专注于推理的混合专家模型，具有700M激活参数和8B总参数，在AMD计算平台上从头训练。该模型采用四阶段RL级联和Markovian RSA测试时计算方法，在数学和编程基准测试上取得了与更大模型相当的性能。

<h3 id="3">[3] Partial Evidence Bench: Benchmarking Authorization-Limited Evidence in Agentic Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.05379](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05379)
- **Authors**: Krti Tallam
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CC; cs.ET
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Benchmark, LLM Security
- **Summary**: 本文提出了Partial Evidence Bench，一个确定性基准测试，用于衡量企业智能体在授权边界限制下产生看似完整但实际缺失重要证据的答案这一失败模式。该基准包含72个任务，涵盖尽职调查、合规审计和安全事件响应三类场景。

<h3 id="4">[4] BALAR : A Bayesian Agentic Loop for Active Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.05386](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05386)
- **Authors**: Aymen Echarghaoui, Dongxia Wu, Emily B. Fox
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, LLM Reasoning, Active Learning
- **Summary**: 本文提出了BALAR，一个任务无关的外循环算法，通过维护潜在状态的结构化信念、最大化期望互信息来选择澄清问题，实现LLM智能体与用户之间的结构化多轮交互。在侦探案件、思维谜题和临床诊断三个基准测试上显著优于所有基线。

<h3 id="5">[5] Intelligent CCTV for Urban Design: AI-Based Analysis of Soft Infrastructure at Intersections</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.05402](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05402)
- **Authors**: Vinit Katariya, Seungjin Kim, Curtis Craig, Nichole Morris, Hamed Tabkhi
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CV; eess.IV
- **Tags**: Computer Vision, Urban Computing, Autonomous Driving
- **Summary**: 本文介绍了一个利用现有CCTV基础设施的AI分析框架，用于评估软性交通干预措施对车速和安全的影响。研究结果显示，在无信号交叉口，平均速度和85分位速度分别下降高达18.75%和16.56%，证明了软性基础设施的交通稳速效果。

<h3 id="6">[6] When Helpfulness Becomes Sycophancy: Sycophancy is a Boundary Failure Between Social Alignment and Epistemic Integrity in Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.05403](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05403)
- **Authors**: Jiechen Li, Catherine A. Barry, Rishika Randev, Janet Chen, Ella Jorgensen, Brinnae Bent
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Alignment, AI Ethics, LLM Evaluation
- **Summary**: 本文认为LLM中的阿谀奉承行为是社会对齐与认知完整性之间的边界失败。作者提出了一个三条件框架来定义阿谀奉承，并引入了由对齐目标、机制和严重程度组成的分类体系，呼吁进行边界感知评估和缓解策略。

<h3 id="7">[7] PRISM: Perception Reasoning Interleaved for Sequential Decision Making</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.05407](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05407)
- **Authors**: Mohamed Salim Aissi, Clemence Grislain, Clement Romac, Laure Soulier, Mohamed Chetouani, Olivier Sigaud, Nicolas Thome
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Embodied AI, Vision-Language Model, LLM Reasoning
- **Summary**: 本文介绍了PRISM框架，通过动态问答管道紧密耦合感知(VLM)和决策(LLM)。LLM批判VLM描述并提出目标导向的问题，生成紧凑的任务驱动场景理解，在ALFWorld和R2R基准测试上显著优于现有图像模型。

<h3 id="8">[8] Agentic Retrieval-Augmented Generation for Financial Document Question Answering</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.05409](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05409)
- **Authors**: Yang Shu, Yingmin Liu, Zequn Xie
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: RAG, Financial AI, Question Answering
- **Summary**: 本文提出了FinAgent-RAG，一个针对金融文档问答的智能体RAG框架，通过迭代检索-推理循环和自验证机制处理复杂的多步数值推理。该框架包含对比金融检索器、程序思维推理模块和自适应策略路由器，在FinQA、ConvFinQA和TAT-QA上显著超越最强基线。

<h3 id="9">[9] LaTA: A Drop-in, FERPA-Compliant Local-LLM Autograder for Upper-Division STEM Coursework</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.05410](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05410)
- **Authors**: Jesse A. Rodríguez
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.HC
- **Tags**: Education Technology, LLM Application, Automated Grading
- **Summary**: 本文介绍了LaTA，一个完全在本地硬件上运行的开源自动评分系统，适用于LaTeX原生的STEM课程作业评分。在俄勒冈州立大学的部署中，该系统实现了低错误率，学生期中和期末考试成绩分别提高了约11%和8%。

<h3 id="10">[10] From History to State: Constant-Context Skill Learning for LLM Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.05413](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05413)
- **Authors**: Haoyang Xie, Xinyuan Wang, Yancheng Wang, Puda Zhao, Feng Ju
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Skill Learning, Memory Architecture
- **Summary**: 本文提出了常量上下文技能学习框架，将可复用的程序学习到轻量级任务族模块中，推理时仅依赖当前观察和紧凑状态块。该方法在ALFWorld、WebShop和SciWorld上取得了强性能，同时将每轮提示令牌减少了2-7倍。

<h3 id="11">[11] The Geopolitics of AI Safety: A Causal Analysis of Regional LLM Bias</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.05427](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05427)
- **Authors**: Alif Al Hasan
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Safety, Fairness, Bias Mitigation
- **Summary**: 本文引入概率图模型框架来因果审计LLM安全机制，通过应用Pearl的do-operator分离注入文化人口统计变量的因果效应。对七个不同来源模型的分析揭示了西方和东方模型在人口统计敏感性上的不同对齐趋势。

<h3 id="12">[12] Authorization Propagation in Multi-Agent AI Systems: Identity Governance as Infrastructure</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.05440](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05440)
- **Authors**: Krti Tallam
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Security, Multi-Agent System, Access Control
- **Summary**: 本文将授权传播形式化为多智能体AI系统中的工作流级属性，识别了三个子问题：传递委托、聚合推理和时间有效性。作者认为身份治理必须被视为基础设施，并推导出授权架构的七个结构要求。

<h3 id="13">[13] Agentic Discovery of Exchange-Correlation Density Functionals</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.05460](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05460)
- **Authors**: Titouan Duston, Jiashu Liang, Yuanheng Wang, Weihao Gao, Xuelan Wen, Nan Sheng, Weiluo Ren, Yang Sun, Yixiao Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Scientific Discovery, Material Discovery
- **Summary**: 本文提出了一个智能体搜索系统，LLM在进化历史指导下提出结构化泛函形式变更，用于发现密度泛函理论中的交换相关泛函。发现的最强泛函SAFS26-a比基线提高约9%，但作者也警示AI可能利用非物理捷径来博弈基准测试。

<h3 id="14">[14] Intentionality is a Design Decision: Measuring Functional Intentionality for Accountable AI Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.05475](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05475)
- **Authors**: Allessia Chiappetta, Robert Mahari
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, AI Safety, AI Governance
- **Summary**: 本文将意图性定义为一种行为特征，包括目的、远见、意志、时间承诺和一致性，并引入功能意图性测试(FIT)来量化智能体系统的意图类行为。该框架使比例监督和自主性校准成为可能。

<h3 id="15">[15] LANTERN: LLM-Augmented Neurosymbolic Transfer with Experience-Gated Reasoning Networks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.05478](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05478)
- **Authors**: Mahyar Alinejad, Yue Wang, Amrit Singh Bedi, George Atia
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Neurosymbolic AI, Transfer Learning, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 本文提出了LANTERN，一个统一的多源神经符号迁移框架，使用LLM从自然语言描述生成任务自动机，并基于语义嵌入自适应聚合多个源策略。该方法在资源管理、导航和控制领域实现了40-60%的样本效率提升。

<h3 id="16">[16] FinRAG-12B: A Production-Validated Recipe for Grounded Question Answering in Banking</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.05482](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05482)
- **Authors**: Denys Katerenchuk, Pablo Duboue, Keelan Evanini, David Gondek, Nithin Govindugari, Olivier Allauzen, Joshua Baptiste, David J More, Joshua Schechter
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.MA
- **Tags**: RAG, Financial AI, Question Answering
- **Venue**: ACL 2026
- **Summary**: 本文提出了一个统一的数据高效框架，用于训练银行领域的接地问答LLM，优化答案质量、引用接地和校准拒绝。该12B模型在引用接地上超越GPT-4.1，已在40多家金融机构部署，查询解决率提高7.1个百分点。

<h3 id="17">[17] FoodCHA: Multi-Modal LLM Agent for Fine-Grained Food Analysis</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.05499](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05499)
- **Authors**: Woojin Lee, Pranav Mekkoth, Ye Tian, Onat Gungor, Tajana Rosing
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Vision-Language Model, Food Computing
- **Summary**: 本文提出FoodCHA，一个多模态智能体框架，将食物识别重新表述为层次化决策过程，通过逐步锚定预测来引导子类别识别和烹饪风格识别。该方法使用紧凑的Moondream-2B视觉语言模型，在FoodNExTDB数据集上显著优于更大的模型。

<h3 id="18">[18] Housing Potential Common Data Model and City Digital Twin</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.05535](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05535)
- **Authors**: Megan Katsumi, Mark Fox, Anderson Wong, Divnoor Chatha
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Digital Twin, Urban Computing, Data Integration
- **Summary**: 本文引入住房潜力通用数据模型(HPCDM)来解决数据孤岛问题，支持住房潜力分析所需的多源数据集整合。研究还创建了住房城市数字孪生和试点仪表板应用，并识别了采用障碍和缓解策略。

<h3 id="19">[19] AgenticRAG: Agentic Retrieval for Enterprise Knowledge Bases</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.05538](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05538)
- **Authors**: Susheel Suresh, Hazel Mak, Shangpo Chou, Fred Kroon, Sahil Bhatnagar
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.IR
- **Tags**: RAG, LLM Agent, Information Retrieval
- **Summary**: 本文提出AgenticRAG，一个用于企业知识库检索和分析的智能体框架，通过为LLM配备搜索、查找、打开和总结工具实现迭代式自主检索。实验显示在BRIGHT、WixQA和FinanceBench基准上相比标准RAG管道有显著提升。

<h3 id="20">[20] SPARK: Self-Play with Asymmetric Reward from Knowledge Graphs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.05546](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05546)
- **Authors**: Hyobin Park, Taeseop Kim, Dong-Geol Choi
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Knowledge Graph, Vision-Language Model, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 本文提出SPARK框架，从多文档科学文献自动构建知识图谱，并将其作为自博弈强化学习的结构基础。小型视觉语言模型在信息不对称下交替扮演提议者和求解者角色，在关系多跳推理任务上表现优异。

<h3 id="21">[21] Who Prices Cognitive Labor in the Age of Agents? A Position on Compute-Anchored Wages</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.05558](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05558)
- **Authors**: Siqi Zhu
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CY
- **Tags**: AI Policy, AI Economics
- **Summary**: 本文论证AI智能体是生产技术而非劳动力，推导出计算锚定工资(CAW)边界，表明在人类与智能体认知劳动可替代的任务上，竞争性人类工资受计算资本租金率约束。该分析对认知劳动经济学具有理论和政策意义。

<h3 id="22">[22] BitCal-TTS: Bit-Calibrated Test-Time Scaling for Quantized Reasoning Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.05561](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05561)
- **Authors**: Sai Babu Patarlapalli, Surya Teja Avvaru
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Inference, Quantization, LLM Reasoning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Saibabu7770/bitcal-tts)
- **Summary**: 本文提出BitCal-TTS，一个用于量化推理模型测试时计算分配的轻量级运行时控制器，解决置信度校准失准问题。该方法结合在线不确定性代理、比特条件置信度重缩放和比特感知确认机制，在GSM8K评估分片上提升了准确率。

<h3 id="23">[23] Nonsense Helps: Prompt Space Perturbation Broadens Reasoning Exploration</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.05566](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05566)
- **Authors**: Langlin Huang, Chengsong Huang, Jinyuan Li, Donghong Cai, Yuyi Yang, Jiaxin Huang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Reinforcement Learning, Prompt Engineering
- **Summary**: 本文提出LoPE训练框架，通过在提示前添加Lorem Ipsum序列来扩展推理探索，解决GRPO中的零优势问题。实验表明该方法在1.7B、4B和7B模型上显著优于原始提示重采样。

<h3 id="24">[24] Locality-aware Private Class Identification for Domain Adaptation with Extreme Label Shift</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.05567](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05567)
- **Authors**: Chuan-Xian Ren, Cheng-Jun Guo, Hong Yan
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Domain Adaptation, Optimal Transport, Transfer Learning
- **Summary**: 本文提出基于最优传输的局部感知私有类别识别方法，用于极端标签偏移下的域适应。该方法引入传输质量分数函数和ReOT方法，在最小化分类风险的同时学习共享类与私有类之间的分离聚类结构。

<h3 id="25">[25] AlphaCrafter: A Full-Stack Multi-Agent Framework for Cross-Sectional Quantitative Trading</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.05580](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05580)
- **Authors**: Yishuo Yuan, Jiayi Sheng, Sirui Zeng, Jiaqi Wang, Jiaheng Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, Quantitative Finance, LLM Agent
- **Summary**: 本文介绍AlphaCrafter，一个全栈多智能体量化交易框架，包含三个专门智能体：用于因子发现的Miner、用于制度条件因子选择的Screener和用于风险约束执行的Trader。在CSI 300和S&P 500上的实验显示优异的风险调整收益。

<h3 id="26">[26] Belief Memory: Agent Memory Under Partial Observability</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.05583](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05583)
- **Authors**: Junfeng Liao, Qizhou Wang, Jianing Zhu, Bo Du, Rui Yan, Xiuying Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Memory Architecture, Uncertainty Estimation
- **Summary**: 本文提出BeliefMem，一种新的记忆范式，存储多个候选结论及其概率而非单一确定性结论，解决LLM智能体在部分可观测环境中的自强化错误问题。该方法使用Noisy-OR规则更新概率，在LoCoMo和ALFWorld基准上表现优异。

<h3 id="27">[27] Causal Probing for Internal Visual Representations in Multimodal Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.05593](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05593)
- **Authors**: Zehao Deng, Tianjie Ju, Zheng Wu, Liangbo He, Jun Lan, Huijia Zhu, Weiqiang Wang, Zhuosheng Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Interpretability, Causal Inference
- **Summary**: 本文提出基于激活引导的因果框架来探测和操控多模态大语言模型的内部视觉表征，揭示实体与抽象概念编码模式的差异。分析发现MLLM在几何关系识别与推理之间存在脱节，未能触发抽象问题求解所需的程序执行。

<h3 id="28">[28] Prober.ai: Gated Inquiry-Based Feedback via LLM-Constrained Personas for Argumentative Writing Development</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.05598](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05598)
- **Authors**: Ran Bi, Shiyao Wei, Yuanyiyi Zhou
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.HC
- **Tags**: Education Technology, LLM Application, Writing Assistance
- **Summary**: 本文呈现Prober.ai，一个基于Web的写作环境，通过约束LLLM仅生成针对性探究问题而非生成文本，实现教学摩擦机制。系统采用挑战和解锁两阶段交互架构，基于Toulmin论证理论设计，在NY EdTech黑客马拉松中获得第二名。

<h3 id="29">[29] Text-Graph Synergy: A Bidirectional Verification and Completion Framework for RAG</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.05643](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05643)
- **Authors**: Jiarui Zhong, Hong Cai Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.IR
- **Tags**: RAG, Knowledge Graph, Multi-Document Reasoning
- **Summary**: 本文提出TGS-RAG统一框架，通过双向机制实现文本-图协同增强：图到文通道用于重排序文本证据，文到图通道用于恢复被剪枝的推理路径。实验显示在多跳推理基准上取得优异性能。

<h3 id="30">[30] Retrieval-Conditioned Topology Selection with Provable Budget Conservation for Multi-Agent Code Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.05657](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05657)
- **Authors**: Abhijit Talluri, Pujith Anne, Bhagavan Choudary Pendiyala, Raghavendra Chilukuri
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.MA
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, Code Generation, LLM Agent
- **Summary**: 本文提出RGAO架构，从代码索引提取结构复杂度向量来选择编排拓扑，实现检索条件下的动态拓扑选择。该方法将复杂度条件LLM路由与形式资源代数结合，提供可证明的预算保守性。

<h3 id="31">[31] Large Vision-Language Models Get Lost in Attention</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.05668](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05668)
- **Authors**: Gongli Xi, Ye Tian, Mengyu Yang, Huahui Yi, Liang Lin, Xiaoshuai Hao, Kun Wang, Wendong Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Interpretability, Transformer Architecture
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 本文提出基于信息论和几何学的统一框架来量化残差更新的几何和熵特性，揭示注意力与FFN的功能解耦。实验表明用预定义值替换学习的注意力权重可获得相当甚至更优的性能，揭示当前机制中的冗余。

<h3 id="32">[32] Chain of Risk: Safety Failures in Large Reasoning Models and Mitigation via Adaptive Multi-Principle Steering</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.05678](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05678)
- **Authors**: Xiaomin Li, Jianheng Hou, Zheyuan Deng, Zhiwei Zhang, Taoran Li, Binghang Lu, Bing Hu, Yunhan Zhao, Yuexing Hao
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Safety, LLM Reasoning, LLM Alignment
- **Summary**: 本文揭示大型推理模型中推理链的安全风险，即使最终答案安全，推理轨迹中仍可能出现有害内容。作者提出自适应多原则引导方法，在保持推理基准准确率的同时减少不安全内容。

<h3 id="33">[33] Attractor Geometry of Transformer Memory: From Conflict Arbitration to Confident Hallucination</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.05686](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05686)
- **Authors**: Qiyao Liang, Risto Miikkulainen, Ila Fiete
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Hallucination, Interpretability, LLM Reasoning
- **Summary**: 本文研究了语言模型中两种失败模式——冲突（参数记忆与工作记忆不一致）和幻觉（事实从未被学习），揭示了两者在隐藏状态空间中共享统一的几何解释：学习到的事实形成吸引子盆地。作者提出几何边界方法能比输出熵更有效地区分正确回忆和幻觉。

<h3 id="34">[34] DataDignity: Training Data Attribution for Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.05687](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05687)
- **Authors**: Xiaomin Li, Andrzej Banburski-Fahey, Jaron Lanier
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Data Attribution, LLM Evaluation, Information Retrieval
- **Summary**: 本文研究训练数据溯源问题，即识别支持模型响应的源文档。作者构建了FakeWiki基准测试，并提出ScoringModel方法，在九个开源LLM和五种查询条件下将平均Recall@10从35.0提升至52.2。

<h3 id="35">[35] GCCM: Enhancing Generative Graph Prediction via Contrastive Consistency Model</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.05689](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05689)
- **Authors**: Shaozhen Ma, Wei Huang, Hanchen Wang, Dong Wen, Wenjie Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Graph Learning, Generative Model, Diffusion Model
- **Summary**: 本文针对图预测中一致性训练可能陷入捷径解的问题，提出GCCM方法，通过引入对比一致性目标和特征扰动来缓解该问题，在图预测任务上取得了优于确定性预测器的性能。

<h3 id="36">[36] Saliency-Aware Regularized Quantization Calibration for Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.05693](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05693)
- **Authors**: Yanlong Zhao, Xiaoyuan Cheng, Huihang Liu, Baihua He, Xinyu Zhang, Harrison Bo Hua Zhu, Wenlong Chen, Li Zeng, Zhuo Sun
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Quantization, Model Compression, LLM Inference
- **Summary**: 本文提出SARQC框架，通过在PTQ校准目标中加入显著性感知正则化项，使量化权重在校准过程中保持接近原始权重，从而提高泛化性能，在密集型和MoE LLM上均显示出一致的改进。

<h3 id="37">[37] Inference-Time Budget Control for LLM Search Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.05701](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05701)
- **Authors**: Zhengru Fang, Senkang Forest Hu, Zhonghao Chang, Yu Guo, Yihang Tao, Hongyao Liu, Mengzhe Ruan, Jun Huang, Yuguang Fang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Question Answering, LLM Inference
- **Summary**: 本文研究LLM搜索代理在双预算约束（工具调用和生成token）下的推理时预算控制问题，提出两阶段方法：搜索时使用信息价值分数选择动作，搜索后使用选择性证据修正答案，在多跳QA基准上取得正向收益。

<h3 id="38">[38] Knowledge-Graph Paths as Intermediate Supervision for Self-Evolving Search Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.05702](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05702)
- **Authors**: Huyu Wu, Jun Liu, Xiaochi Wei, Yan Gao, Yi Wu, Yao Hu
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Knowledge Graph, Question Answering
- **Summary**: 本文通过重用知识图谱路径作为中间监督来改进自进化搜索代理，为问题构建提供关系上下文，并引入路径点覆盖奖励为错误轨迹提供部分奖励，在七个QA基准上均优于标准SSP方法。

<h3 id="39">[39] Resolving the bias-precision paradox with stochastic causal representation learning for personalized medicine</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.05706](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05706)
- **Authors**: Peisong Zhang, Manqiang Peng, Yuxuan Wu, Pawit Phadungsaksawasdi, Wesley Yeung, Ye Zhang, Trang Nguyen, Qiang Zhang, Nan Liu, Meng Wang, Kee Yuan Ngiam, Yih-Chung Tham, Ching-Yu Cheng, Tianfan Fu, Qingyu Chen, Rosemary Ke, Chang Li, Wenzhuo Yang, Zhenghao Lu, Chunyou Lai, Yu Zhang, Sheng Zhong, Hao Deng, Dianbo Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; q-bio.QM
- **Tags**: Causal Representation Learning, Medical AI, Treatment Effect Estimation
- **Summary**: 本文识别了因果表示学习中的偏差-精度悖论，提出sMMD随机对齐策略替代全局对抗平衡，在两个大规模ICU队列上提高了分布偏移下的准确性，并支持可解释的实时临床决策。

<h3 id="40">[40] Conceal, Reconstruct, Jailbreak: Exploiting the Reconstruction-Concealment Tradeoff in MLLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.05709](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05709)
- **Authors**: Md Farhamdur Reza, Richeng Jin, Tianfu Wu, Huaiyu Dai
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Security, Jailbreak Attack, Vision-Language Model
- **Summary**: 本文研究MLLM中基于意图混淆的越狱攻击，发现现有方法难以平衡重构-隐藏权衡。作者提出隐藏感知变体构建和关键词相关干扰图像策略，在开源和闭源MLLM上均优于强基线。

<h3 id="41">[41] Decodable but Not Corrected by Fixed Residual-Stream Linear Steering: Evidence from Medical LLM Failure Regimes</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.05715](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05715)
- **Authors**: Ming Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Interpretability, LLM Evaluation, Medical AI
- **Summary**: 本文研究医学QA中LLM隐藏状态的线性可解码失败信号能否用于纠正失败。研究发现虽然"过度思考"失败模式可被解码，但由于表示纠缠，固定线性引导无法纠正，但可用于选择性弃权。

<h3 id="42">[42] More Is Not Always Better: Cross-Component Interference in LLM Agent Scaffolding</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.05716](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05716)
- **Authors**: Ming Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, LLM Evaluation, Multi-Agent System
- **Summary**: 本文研究LLM代理系统中的跨组件干扰问题，通过全因子实验发现添加更多脚手架组件可能导致性能下降，最优组件数量取决于任务和模型规模，All-In系统始终次优。

<h3 id="43">[43] Detecting Time Series Anomalies Like an Expert: A Multi-Agent LLM Framework with Specialized Analyzers</h3>

- **Authors**: Hyeongwon Kang, Jeongseob Kim, Jinwoo Park, Pilsung Kang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Anomaly Detection, Time Series Analysis, Multi-Agent System
- **Summary**: 本文提出SAGE多代理框架用于时间序列异常检测，将异常分析分解为四个专门分析器（点、结构、季节、模式），生成结构化诊断报告，在三个基准上取得最佳平均性能。

<h3 id="44">[44] SkillRet: A Large-Scale Benchmark for Skill Retrieval in LLM Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.05726](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05726)
- **Authors**: Hongcheol Cho, Ryangkyung Kang, Youngeun Kim
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Information Retrieval, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 本文引入SkillRet大规模基准测试，包含17,810个代理技能和评估查询，用于LLM代理的技能检索研究。实验表明现有检索器在真实大规模技能库上表现不佳，任务特定微调可显著提升性能。

<h3 id="45">[45] Knee Osteoarthritis Severity Grading Using Optimized Deep Learning and LLM-Driven Intelligent AI on Computationally Limited Systems</h3>

- **Authors**: Dayam Nadeem, Neha, Safdar Mustafa, Adnan Alvi, Mohd Hussain
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical Imaging, Medical AI, DNN Deployment
- **Venue**: ICECI 2026
- **Summary**: 本文提出一种膝骨关节炎严重程度分级方法，结合CNN深度学习模型和TensorFlow Lite实现设备端部署，并使用LLM生成解释性发现，模型测试准确率达94.48%。

<h3 id="46">[46] SDFlow: Similarity-Driven Flow Matching for Time Series Generation</h3>

- **Authors**: Wei Li, Shibo Feng, Pengcheng Wu, Min Wu, Peilin Zhao
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Time Series Generation, Flow Matching, Generative Model
- **Summary**: 本文提出SDFlow非自回归框架用于时间序列生成，通过流匹配在VQ潜在空间实现并行序列生成，解决了自回归模型的曝光偏差问题，在长序列生成上达到最优性能并显著加速推理。

<h3 id="47">[47] ReFlect: An Effective Harness System for Complex Long-Horizon LLM Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.05737](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05737)

- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Self-Correction, Error Detection
- **Summary**: 本文提出ReFlect推理框架系统，为LLM创建独立的错误检测和恢复逻辑。在六个推理领域实验中，该方法显著提高了任务成功率，并在SWE-bench上将补丁结构质量从0%提升至82-87%。

<h3 id="48">[48] HyperLens: Quantifying Cognitive Effort in LLMs with Fine-grained Confidence Trajectory</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.05741](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05741)
- **Authors**: Chengda Lu, Xiaoyu Fan, Wei Xu
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Interpretability, LLM Evaluation, Fine-Tuning
- **Summary**: 本文引入HyperLens高分辨率探针，通过追踪置信度轨迹来量化LLM推理过程中的认知努力。研究发现复杂任务需要更高的认知努力，并揭示了标准监督微调可能降低认知努力从而导致性能下降。

<h3 id="49">[49] Best Arm Identification in Generalized Linear Bandits via Hybrid Feedback</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.05745](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05745)
- **Authors**: Qirun Zeng, Xuchuang Wang, Jiayi Shen, Xutong Liu, Fang Kong, Jinhang Zuo
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Armed Bandit, Reinforcement Learning, Optimization
- **Summary**: 本文研究广义线性老虎机中的固定置信度最佳臂识别问题，提出了一种混合反馈模型，结合绝对奖励反馈和相对（对比）反馈。作者设计了基于似然比的置信序列和Track-and-Stop算法，在成本感知设置下显著提高了样本效率。

<h3 id="50">[50] Evaluating Explainability in Safety-Critical ATR Systems: Limitations of Post-Hoc Methods and Paths Toward Robust XAI</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.05748](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05748)
- **Authors**: Vanessa Buhrmester, David Muench, Dimitri Bulatov, Michael Arens
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Explainable AI, Adversarial Robustness, Object Detection
- **Venue**: ICPR Workshop
- **Summary**: 本文对安全关键自动目标识别（ATR）系统中的可解释性方法进行了结构化评估，识别了现有事后解释方法的系统性局限性。研究提出了一个分类体系，从可解释性、鲁棒性、抗操纵能力和验证适用性四个维度评估XAI方法，并指出了虚假解释、扰动不稳定性等关键失效模式。

<h3 id="51">[51] Confidence is the key: how conformal prediction enhances the generative design of permeable peptides</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Molecular Generation, Reinforcement Learning, Uncertainty Estimation
- **Summary**: 本文提出了一个结合强化学习和共形预测的生成框架，用于设计可渗透的环肽分子。该方法通过不确定性感知的渗透性预测器和共形预测来解决预测不确定性问题，提高了肽优化的可靠性和效率，同时避免在预测器适用域之外进行探索。

<h3 id="52">[52] CircuitFormer: A Circuit Language Model for Analog Topology Design from Natural Language Prompt</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.05773](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05773)

- **Subjects**: cs.AI

- **Summary**: 本文提出了CircuitFormer，一个用于从自然语言提示设计模拟电路拓扑的电路专用语言模型。作者构建了最大的模拟电路网表数据集，并设计了新型电路图分词器CKT，在语法正确性和功能成功率上显著优于现有开源LLM。

<h3 id="53">[53] HEDP: A Hybrid Energy-Distance Prompt-based Framework for Domain Incremental Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.05776](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05776)

- **Tags**: LLM Inference, KV Cache, Long Context
- **Summary**: 本文提出了SPEED方法，通过层非对称KV可见性策略，仅在低层保留预填充token的KV状态，在保持基准性能的同时显著降低了长上下文推理的时间和内存成本。

<h3 id="87">[87] On Time, Within Budget: Constraint-Driven Online Resource Allocation for Agentic Workflows</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.06110](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06110)

- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL

- **Summary**: 本文研究了智能体工作流的约束驱动在线资源分配问题，提出了蒙特卡洛组合规划(MCPP)方法，在预算和截止时间约束下最大化工作流成功完成的概率。

<h3 id="88">[88] CrossCult-KIBench: A Benchmark for Cross-Cultural Knowledge Insertion in MLLMs</h3>

<h3 id="89">[89] Policy-Guided Stepwise Model Routing for Cost-Effective Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.06116](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06116)
- **Authors**: Wenwen Si, Insup Lee, Osbert Bastani

<h3 id="90">[90] Back to the Beginning of Heuristic Design: Bridging Code and Knowledge with LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.06123](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06123)

<h3 id="91">[91] P-Guide: Parameter-Efficient Prior Steering for Single-Pass CFG Inference</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.06124](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06124)

- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Diffusion Model, Flow Matching, Image Generation
- **Summary**: 本文提出了P-Guide框架，通过仅调制初始潜在状态实现单次推理的高质量引导，在保持与双通道CFG相当的保真度和提示对齐的同时，将推理延迟降低约50%。

<h3 id="92">[92] Skill1: Unified Evolution of Skill-Augmented Agents via Reinforcement Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.06130](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06130)

- **Summary**: 本文提出了Skill1框架，通过单一任务结果信号训练策略，使技能选择、利用和蒸馏三种能力协同进化，在ALFWorld和WebShop上优于现有技能增强和强化学习基线。

<h3 id="93">[93] Graphlets as Building Blocks for Structural Vocabulary in Knowledge Graph Foundation Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.06154](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06154)

- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG

<h3 id="94">[94] Beyond Accuracy: Policy Invariance as a Reliability Test for LLM Safety Judges</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.06161](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06161)
- **Authors**: Shihao Weng, Yang Feng, Xiaofei Xie
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.SE
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, AI Safety, LLM Agent
- **Summary**: 本文提出了策略不变性作为LLM安全评估器的可靠性测试，包括三个可测试原则，揭示了现有评估器无法区分有意义的规范变化和无意义结构重写的问题。

<h3 id="95">[95] Post Reasoning: Improving the Performance of Non-Thinking Models at No Cost</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.06165](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06165)
- **Authors**: Richmond Sin Jing Xuan, Rishabh Bhardwaj, Soujanya Poria
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Instruction Tuning, Prompt Engineering
- **Summary**: 本文提出了后推理方法，通过让模型在生成最终答案后进行论证来提升性能，在117个模型-基准设置中88.19%的情况下实现了改进，且不增加推理延迟。

<h3 id="96">[96] BioMedArena: An Open-source Toolkit for Building and Evaluating Biomedical Deep Research Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.06177](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06177)
- **Authors**: Jinge Wu, Hongjian Zhou, Mingde Zeng, Jiayuan Zhu, Junde Wu, Jiazhen Pan, Sean Wu, Honghan Wu, Fenglin Liu, David A. Clifton
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, LLM Agent, Benchmark
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/AI-in-Health/BioMedArena)
- **Summary**: 本文发布了BioMedArena开源工具包，解耦了生物医学智能体评估的六个层次，提供147个基准和75个工具，在8个代表性生物医学基准上实现了最先进结果。

<h3 id="97">[97] Rethinking Adapter Placement: A Dominant Adaptation Module Perspective</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.06183](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06183)
- **Authors**: Suoxin Zhang, Run He, Di Fang, Xiang Tan, Kaixuan Chen, Huiping Zhuang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning, Model Compression, LLM Inference
- **Summary**: 本文研究LoRA适配器在预训练模型中的最优放置问题，提出PAGE方法识别出单一浅层FFN下投影层为"主导适配模块"。基于此发现，作者提出DomLoRA方法，仅使用约0.7%的参数量即可在指令遵循、数学推理、代码生成等任务上超越标准LoRA。

<h3 id="98">[98] Event-Causal RAG: A Retrieval-Augmented Generation Framework for Long Video Reasoning in Complex Scenarios</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.06185](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06185)
- **Authors**: Peizheng Yan, Yu Zhao, Liang Xie, Juntong Qi, Mingming Wang, Erwei Yin
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: RAG, Video Understanding, Vision-Language Model
- **Summary**: 本文提出Event-Causal RAG框架用于长视频推理，将视频分割为语义连贯的事件并构建状态-事件-状态图。该框架通过双存储记忆支持语义匹配和因果拓扑检索，在长视频理解基准上显著优于基于片段的检索基线，同时提高内存效率。

<h3 id="99">[99] OPSD Compresses What RLVR Teaches: A Post-RL Compaction Stage for Reasoning Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.06188](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06188)
- **Authors**: Jaehoon Kim, Dongha Lee
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Knowledge Distillation, Mathematical Reasoning
- **Summary**: 本文研究发现On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD)在思维链启用的数学推理中更适合作为压缩机制而非纠错机制。作者提出改进的训练流程：SFT→RLVR→OPSD，仅在正确rollout上训练OPSD以保持准确率同时缩短响应长度。

<h3 id="100">[100] Systematic Evaluation of Large Language Models for Post-Discharge Clinical Action Extraction</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.06191](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06191)
- **Authors**: Shivali Dalmia, Ananya Mantravadi, Prasanna Desikan
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Information Extraction, LLM Evaluation
- **Summary**: 本文系统评估了LLM在出院小结临床行动提取任务上的表现，提出两阶段提取框架将叙述性文本分解为可执行的临床任务。研究表明LLM在二元可操作性检测上达到监督模型水平，但在细粒度分类上仍有差距，并指出需要带有推理标注的数据集来正确评估临床理解能力。

<h3 id="101">[101] The Granularity Axis: A Micro-to-Macro Latent Direction for Social Roles in Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.06196](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06196)
- **Authors**: Chonghan Qin, Xiachong Feng, Ziyun Song, Xiaocheng Feng, Jing Xiong, Lingpeng Kong
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Interpretability, Representation Learning, LLM Evaluation
- **Summary**: 本文发现LLM内部表示编码了社会角色的粒度轴（从微观个体到宏观机构），该轴在Qwen3-8B中与角色表示空间的主成分对齐并解释52.6%的方差。研究表明该粒度轴具有因果相关性，激活引导可按预测方向改变响应粒度。

<h3 id="102">[102] Towards Annotation-Free Validation of MLLMs: A Vision-Language Logical Consistency Metric</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.06201](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06201)
- **Authors**: Ying Gu, Mei Chee Leong, Hui Li Tan, Shangbo Mao, Liyuan Li, Nancy Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, LLM Evaluation, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 本文提出VL-LCM（视觉-语言逻辑一致性度量），用于在无需真实标注的情况下评估多模态LLM。该方法基于充分和必要因果关系的逻辑原则，实验表明尽管MLLM准确率提升显著，逻辑一致性仍明显滞后，可用于无标注场景下的模型验证和选择。

<h3 id="103">[103] Beyond Fixed Benchmarks and Worst-Case Attacks: Dynamic Boundary Evaluation for Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.06213](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06213)
- **Authors**: Haoxiang Wang, Da Yu, Huishuai Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Benchmark, LLM Safety
- **Summary**: 本文提出动态边界评估(DBE)方法，主动定位每个模型通过概率接近0.5的边界点，而非使用固定基准测试。DBE提供校准的项目库、技能引导边界搜索算法和自适应评估协议，覆盖安全、能力和真实性四个类别，避免了固定基准的饱和效应。

<h3 id="104">[104] Joint Consistency: A Unified Test-Time Aggregation Framework via Energy Minimization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.06219](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06219)
- **Authors**: Yunzhen Yao, Hongye Wang, Yahong Wang, Michael C. Gastpar, Bo Jiang, Lie He
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Test-Time Adaptation, Uncertainty Estimation
- **Summary**: 本文提出联合一致性(JC)框架，将测试时聚合问题形式化为约束Ising型能量最小化问题。JC结合独立评估信号和候选轨迹间的成对比较，在数学和代码推理基准上一致优于现有投票和加权聚合方法。

<h3 id="105">[105] Proactive Instance Navigation with Comparative Judgment for Ambiguous User Queries</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.06223](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06223)
- **Authors**: Junhyuk Kwon, Seungjoon Lee, Hyejin Park, Kyle Min, Jungseul Ok
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.RO
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Question Answering, Information Retrieval
- **Summary**: 本文提出ProCompNav框架处理模糊用户查询的实例导航问题，通过比较判断在候选池中识别目标。该方法每轮提取能分割候选池的属性-值对并询问二元问题，将消歧从开放式描述重构为池级判别式提问，显著提高成功率并减少响应长度。

<h3 id="106">[106] A Versatile AI Agent for Rare Disease Diagnosis and Risk Gene Prioritization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.06226](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06226)
- **Authors**: Tianyu Liu, Wangjie Zheng, Rui Yang, Benny Kai Guo Loo, Hui Zhang, Jeffries Lauran, Jianlei Gu, Botao Yu, Weihao Xuan, Kexin Huang, Nan Liu, James Zou, Yonghui Jiang, Hua Xu, Hongyu Zhao
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; q-bio.GN
- **Tags**: Medical AI, LLM Agent, Drug Discovery
- **Summary**: 本文介绍Hygieia多模态AI代理系统，用于罕见病诊断和风险基因优先排序。该系统采用基于路由器和知识增强的框架来减少幻觉，整合表型特征、基因档案和临床记录，在多个诊断基准上达到最先进性能，并经临床专家验证其实用性。

<h3 id="107">[107] Price of Fairness in Short-Term and Long-Term Algorithmic Selections</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.06227](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06227)
- **Authors**: Shahin Jabbari, Chen Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Fairness, Decision Making, Algorithm Selection
- **Venue**: IJCAI 2026
- **Summary**: 本文研究序贯选择问题中公平性与效用之间的权衡，分析短期和长期公平性概念。研究表明短期公平性代价即使在群体分布相似时也可能很大，而长期差异在简单投资策略下可以消失且公平性代价较低。

<h3 id="108">[108] Safactory: A Scalable Agent Factory for Trustworthy Autonomous Intelligence</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.06230](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06230)
- **Authors**: Xinquan Chen, Zhenyun Yin, Shan He, Bin Huang, Shanzhe Lei, Pengcheng Shi, Kun Cai, Bei Chen, Bangwei Liu, Zeyu Kang, Chao Huang, Yang Zhang, Wenjie Li, Ruijun Ge, Yajie Wang, Tianshun Fang, Tianyang Xu, Yiwen Cong, Meng Jin, Gaolei Li, Xuansheng Wu, Linhan Liu, Zijing He, An Li, Yan Teng, Xin Tan, ChaoChao Lu, Ji He, Jie Li, Chunfeng Song, Jinya Xu, Fan Song, Shujie Wang, Jianmin Qian, Jie Hou, Xuhong Wang, Yingchun Wang, Hui Wang, Xia Hu
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.DC
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Multi-Agent System, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 本文提出Safactory可扩展代理工厂框架，用于可信自主智能系统。该框架集成并行仿真平台、可信数据平台和自主进化平台，实现轨迹生成、经验提取和异步强化学习的闭环持续改进，是首个提出统一进化管道的框架。

<h3 id="109">[109] Data Language Models: A New Foundation Model Class for Tabular Data</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.06290](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06290)
- **Authors**: Eda Erol, Giuliano Pezzoli, Ozer Cem Kelahmet
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Foundation Model, Tabular Learning, Data Integration
- **Summary**: 本文引入数据语言模型(DLM)作为表格数据的基础模型类，可直接理解原始单元格值而无需预处理。Schema-1作为首个DLM在230万数据集上训练，在行级预测基准上优于梯度提升集成和AutoML，并能从原始单元格值识别行业领域。

<h3 id="110">[110] Addressing Labelled Data Scarcity: Taxonomy-Agnostic Annotation of PII Values in HTTP Traffic using LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.06305](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06305)
- **Authors**: Thomas Cory, Axel Küpper
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.IR
- **Tags**: Privacy, Data Annotation, LLM Application
- **Venue**: EuroS&PW 2026
- **Summary**: 本文研究LLM在HTTP流量中PII值标注的应用，提出多阶段LLM管道结合确定性预处理、标签级分类和实例级标注。该方法支持运行时提供分类法，实现跨领域和演进PII定义的灵活标注，并引入合成HTTP流量生成器用于评估。

<h3 id="111">[111] Measuring Black-Box Confidence via Reasoning Trajectories: Geometry, Coverage, and Verbalization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.06308](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06308)
- **Authors**: Marc Boubnovski Martell, Josefa Lia Stoisser, Kaspar Märtens, Jialin Yu, Robert Kitchen, Philip Torr, Jesper Ferkinghoff-Borg
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Uncertainty Estimation, LLM Evaluation
- **Summary**: 本文提出黑盒轨迹置信度评分方法，将思维链嵌入为滑动窗口轨迹并测量其收敛性。该方法融合几何、覆盖率和语言化三个通道，在K=4时达到比K=8自一致性更好的置信度估计效果，无需logits或隐藏状态。

<h3 id="112">[112] A Regime Theory of Controller Class Selection for LLM Action Decisions</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.06339](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06339)
- **Authors**: Zhaoyang Jiang, Zhizhong Fu, Yunsoo Kim, Jiacong Mi, Zicheng Li, Xuanqi Peng, Honghan Wu
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Decision Making, LLM Inference
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Anonymous-Awesome-Submissions/Regime-Theory)
- **Summary**: 本文提出控制器类选择的制度理论，将控制器组织为四类嵌套格：固定动作、分区路由、实例级控制器和先验门控控制器。理论基于三个数据可估计瓶颈确定最优控制器类，并通过严格交叉验证证明可近似选择最佳类别。

<h3 id="113">[113] Mind the Gap? A Distributional Comparison of Real and Synthetic Priors for Tabular Foundation Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.06343](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06343)
- **Authors**: Alex O. Davies, Telmo de Menezes e Silva Filho, Nirav Ajmeri
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Tabular Learning, Foundation Model, Data Synthesis
- **Summary**: 本文比较了表格基础模型的三类预训练语料库（网络爬取、精选数据集和合成数据），发现合成先验仅占据真实表格分布的狭窄区域，而精选和网络爬取语料在分布层面可互换。令人意外的是，合成预训练数据与真实表格之间的分布差距对下游性能没有明显影响。

<h3 id="114">[114] More Than Can Be Said: A Benchmark and Framework for Pre-Question Scientific Ideation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.06345](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06345)
- **Authors**: Jie Yu, Song Qiu
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Scientific Discovery, Benchmark
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Paradoxtcal/InciteResearch.git)
- **Summary**: 本文提出了InciteResearch多智能体框架，通过苏格拉底式提问将研究者的隐性理解转化为显式、可检验的研究问题，并发布了TF-Bench基准测试。实验表明该方法在新颖性和影响力指标上显著超越基线，将生成提案从简单重组提升到架构洞察层面。

<h3 id="115">[115] Prediction and Empowerment: A Theory of Agency through Bridge Interfaces</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.06346](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06346)
- **Authors**: Richard Csaky
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: AI Safety, Decision Making, Theory
- **Summary**: 本文提出了一个关于部分可观测确定性世界中智能体的理论框架，将感知和行动建模为桥接接口。作者证明了预测、压缩和赋能之间的分离关系，并为现代AI智能体设计提供了区分隐状态识别、接口细化和任务相关可控性的原则。

<h3 id="116">[116] From Agent Loops to Deterministic Graphs: Execution Lineage for Reproducible AI-Native Work</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.06365](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06365)
- **Authors**: Josh Rosen, Seth Rosen
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.MA; cs.SE
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Workflow Automation, Research Reproducibility
- **Summary**: 本文提出了执行谱系概念，将AI原生工作表示为具有显式依赖关系的DAG结构，提供稳定的中间边界和基于身份的重放能力。实验表明DAG重放在策略备忘录更新任务中优于循环式基线，能够完美保留无关分支并正确传播修改。

<h3 id="117">[117] Debiased Multimodal Personality Understanding through Dual Causal Intervention</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.06371](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06371)
- **Authors**: Yangfu Zhu, Zitong Han, Nianwen Ning, Yuting Wei, Yuandong Wang, Hang Feng, Zhenzhou Shao
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multimodal Learning, Causal Inference, Fairness
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Sabrina-han/DCAN)
- **Summary**: 本文提出了双因果调整网络(DCAN)来缓解多模态人格理解中的主体偏差问题，通过后门调整和前门调整模块实现因果解耦。作者还构建了带人口统计标注的多模态学生人格数据集(DMSP)，在基准数据集上实现了准确率和公平性指标的双重提升。

<h3 id="118">[118] Rethinking Vacuity for OOD Detection in Evidential Deep Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.06382](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06382)
- **Authors**: Claire McNamara
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Out-of-Distribution Detection, Uncertainty Estimation, Model Evaluation
- **Summary**: 本文指出证据深度学习中基于空度的分布外检测方法对类别基数差异高度敏感，当ID和OOD类别数不同时AUROC和AUPR会被人为夸大。作者通过实验证明类别基数差异可导致AUROC差异高达0.360，呼吁在评估中明确控制这一因素。

<h3 id="119">[119] Automated alignment is harder than you think</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.06390](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06390)
- **Authors**: Aleksandr Bowkis, Marie Davidsen Buhl, Jacob Pfau, Geoffrey Irving
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: AI Safety, LLM Alignment, Automated Alignment
- **Summary**: 本文论证了自动化对齐研究可能产生具有误导性的安全评估结果，因为对齐研究涉及大量难以监督的模糊任务。作者指出AI生成的对齐方案可能包含人类无法评估的论证，且优化压力会使错误集中在人类评审者最难以发现的区域。

<h3 id="120">[120] Knowledge Graphs, the Missing Link in Agentic AI-based Formal Verification</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.06434](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06434)

<h3 id="121">[121] SCRuB: Social Concept Reasoning under Rubric-Based Evaluation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.06444](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06444)

<h3 id="122">[122] PrefixGuard: From LLM-Agent Traces to Online Failure-Warning Monitors</h3>

<h3 id="123">[123] Beyond Task Success: Measuring Workflow Fidelity in LLM-Based Agentic Payment Systems</h3>

<h3 id="124">[124] Probabilistic Dating of Historical Manuscripts via Evidential Deep Regression on Visual Script Features</h3>

<h3 id="125">[125] Patch-Effect Graph Kernels for LLM Interpretability</h3>

<h3 id="126">[126] ReasonSTL: Bridging Natural Language and Signal Temporal Logic via Tool-Augmented Process-Rewarded Learning</h3>

<h3 id="127">[127] Instrumental Choices: Measuring the Propensity of LLM Agents to Pursue Instrumental Behaviors</h3>

<h3 id="129">[129] Process Matters more than Output for Distinguishing Humans from Machines</h3>

- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/AhmedHamadto/TSA)
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了Token选择性注意力(TSA)机制，通过学习的逐token门控实现Transformer的自适应计算深度，在保持质量损失<0.5%的情况下节省了14-23%的token层操作。

<h3 id="150">[150] Structural Instability of Feature Composition</h3>

<h3 id="151">[151] Channel-Level Semantic Perturbations: Unlearnable Examples for Diverse Training Paradigms</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.05224](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05224) (cross-listed)

<h3 id="152">[152] MACS: Modality-Aware Capacity Scaling for Efficient Multimodal MoE Inference</h3>

- **Summary**: 该论文提出了MACS框架，通过熵加权负载机制和动态模态自适应容量分配，解决了多模态MoE LLM在专家并行推理中的负载均衡问题，无需训练即可显著提升推理效率。

<h3 id="153">[153] Internalizing Outcome Supervision into Process Supervision: A New Paradigm for Reinforcement Learning for Reasoning</h3>

<h3 id="154">[154] Rethinking Data Curation in LLM Training: Online Reweighting Offers Better Generalization than Offline Methods</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.05227](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05227) (cross-listed)

<h3 id="155">[155] Evolutionary fine tuning of quantized convolution-based deep learning models</h3>

<h3 id="156">[156] Topology-Driven Anti-Entanglement Control for Soft Robots</h3>

<h3 id="157">[157] PPO-Based Dynamic Positioning of HAPS-BS in Wind-Disturbed Stratospheric Maritime Networks</h3>

<h3 id="158">[158] Beyond Semantic Similarity: Rethinking Retrieval for Agentic Search via Direct Corpus Interaction</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.05242](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05242) (cross-listed)

<h3 id="159">[159] Towards Dependable Retrieval-Augmented Generation Using Factual Confidence Prediction</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.05244](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05244) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI

- **Summary**: 该论文提出了一种两阶段方法来预测RAG系统的事实可信度，首先使用保形预测选择高概率正确的检索块，然后用基于注意力的事实性分类器量化生成答案与检索上下文的一致性。

<h3 id="160">[160] Memory-Efficient EDA Denoising via Knowledge Distillation for Wearable IoT Under Severe Motion Artifacts and Underwater Conditions</h3>

<h3 id="161">[161] Governed Metaprogramming for Intelligent Systems: Reclassifying Eval as a Governed Effec</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.05248](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05248) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.PL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: AI Safety, LLM Agent, Formal Methods

- **Summary**: 本文提出了一种“受控元编程”的语言设计，将eval从语言原语重新归类为受控效应，使得程序表示到可执行机器的转换过程受到结构检查和策略合规性的约束。该设计在MashinTalk DSL中实现，并证明了形式操作的纯度、不可绕过定理和边界保持等属性。

<h3 id="162">[162] Decision-aware User Simulation Agent for Evaluating Conversational Recommender Systems</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI

<h3 id="163">[163] Automated Population-Level Audit Assurance via AI-Based Document Intelligence</h3>

<h3 id="164">[164] Career-Aware Resume Tailoring via Multi-Source Retrieval-Augmented Generation with Provenance Tracking: A Case Study</h3>

<h3 id="165">[165] Enhancing Cryo-EM Density Map Segmentation in Phenix for Improved Atomic Model Building</h3>

<h3 id="166">[166] Maximizing Rollout Informativeness under a Fixed Budget: A Submodular View of Tree Search for Tool-Use Agentic Reinforcement Learning</h3>

<h3 id="167">[167] Bridging Generation and Training: A Systematic Review of Quality Issues in LLMs for Code</h3>

<h3 id="168">[168] Shattering the Echo Chamber: Hidden Safeguards in Manuscripts Against the AI Takeover of Peer Review</h3>

<h3 id="169">[169] Securing the Agent: Vendor-Neutral, Multitenant Enterprise Retrieval and Tool Use</h3>

<h3 id="170">[170] Graph Normalization: Fast Binarizing Dynamics for Differentiable MWIS</h3>

<h3 id="171">[171] ViTok-v2: Scaling Native Resolution Auto-Encoders to 5 Billion Parameters</h3>

<h3 id="172">[172] How Far Are VLMs from Privacy Awareness in the Physical World? An Empirical Study</h3>

<h3 id="174">[174] Open-SAT: LLM-Guided Query Embedding Refinement for Open-Vocabulary Object Retrieval in Satellite Imagery</h3>

<h3 id="175">[175] Making AI Drafts Count: A Quality Threshold in Audio Description Workflows</h3>

- **Authors**: Huchen Yang, Xinghao Dong, Dan Negrut, Jin-Long Wu
- **Subjects**: cs.MA; cs.AI; cs.LG

<h3 id="215">[215] SafeHarbor: Hierarchical Memory-Augmented Guardrail for LLM Agent Safety</h3>

<h3 id="216">[216] Auto Research with Specialist Agents Develops Effective and Non-Trivial Training Recipes</h3>

<h3 id="217">[217] WARP: A Benchmark for Primal-Dual Warm-Starting of Interior-Point Solvers</h3>

<h3 id="218">[218] CRAFT: Forgetting-Aware Intervention-Based Adaptation for Continual Learning</h3>

<h3 id="219">[219] CoMemNet: Contrastive Sampling with Memory Replay Network for Continual Traffic Prediction</h3>

<h3 id="220">[220] Multi-Dimensional Behavioral Evaluation of Agentic Stock Prediction Systems Using LLM Judges with Closed-Loop Reinforcement Learning Feedback</h3>

<h3 id="222">[222] Transformers Provably Implement In-Context Reinforcement Learning with Policy Improvement</h3>

<h3 id="245">[245] Towards Reliable LLM Evaluation: Correcting the Winner's Curse in Adaptive Benchmarking</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.05973](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05973) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yang Xu, Jiefu Zhang, Haixiang Sun, Zihan Zhou, Tianyu Cao, Vaneet Aggarwal
- **Subjects**: stat.ML; cs.AI; cs.LG; stat.AP
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Benchmark, Uncertainty Estimation
- **Summary**: 本文提出SIREN协议，通过选择感知的重复分割报告方法解决自适应基准测试中的赢家诅咒问题，为LLM评估提供有效的置信区间和不确定性量化。

<h3 id="246">[246] PragLocker: Protecting Agent Intellectual Property in Untrusted Deployments via Non-Portable Prompts</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.05974](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05974) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Qinfeng Li, Yuntai Bao, Jianghui Hu, Wenqi Zhang, Jintao Chen, Huifeng Zhu, Yier Jin, Xuhong Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, LLM Security, Prompt Engineering
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 本文提出PragLocker方案，通过构建功能保持的混淆提示来保护LLM代理的知识产权，使提示仅在目标LLM上有效，防止在不可信部署中被复制和跨模型移植。

<h3 id="247">[247] iPhoneBlur: A Difficulty-Stratified Benchmark for Consumer Device Motion Deblurring</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.05990](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05990) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Abdullah Al Shafi, Kazi Saeed Alam
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Image Restoration, Benchmark, Mobile AI
- **Summary**: 本文引入iPhoneBlur基准数据集，包含7400对从iPhone 17 Pro高帧率视频合成的图像对，按难度分层评估运动去模糊，揭示了聚合指标掩盖的7-9 dB性能差异。

<h3 id="248">[248] Safety Anchor: Defending Harmful Fine-tuning via Geometric Bottlenecks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.05995](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05995) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Alignment, LLM Security, Fine-Tuning
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 本文提出安全瓶颈正则化(SBR)方法，通过将有害查询的最终隐藏状态锚定到安全对齐模型来防御有害微调攻击，仅需单个安全锚点即可将有害评分降至10以下。

<h3 id="249">[249] A Fine-Grained Understanding of Uniform Convergence for Halfspaces</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.06004](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06004) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Aryeh Kontorovich, Kasper Green Larsen
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; math.ST
- **Tags**: Deep Learning Theory, Generalization Bound
- **Summary**: 本文对半空间的均匀收敛行为进行细粒度分析，证明非齐次半空间的标准VC界是紧的，而二维齐次半空间在可实现情况下误差为O(1/n)，揭示了维度和结构阈值。

<h3 id="250">[250] PersonaKit (PK): A Plug-and-Play Platform for User Testing Diverse Roles in Full-Duplex Dialogue</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.06007](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06007) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.HC

- **Summary**: 本文提出PersonaKit开源平台，用于快速原型设计和评估全双工对话代理，支持定义多样化角色和可定制的中断处理行为，便于研究下一代语音代理的社会语言学行为。

<h3 id="251">[251] Adding Thermal Awareness to Visual Systems in Real-Time via Distilled Diffusion Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.06010](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06010) (cross-listed)

- **Summary**: 本文提出FusionProxy实时图像融合模块，通过蒸馏扩散模型将热成像感知添加到RGB视觉系统中，在夜间和雾天等挑战性场景下实现鲁棒感知，支持从高端GPU到普通硬件的实时推理。

<h3 id="252">[252] T2I-VeRW: Part-level Fine-grained Perception for Text-to-Image Vehicle Retrieval</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.06012](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06012) (cross-listed)

- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Event-AHU/Neuromorphic_ReID)
- **Summary**: 本文提出PFCVR模型用于文本到图像车辆重识别，通过部件级细粒度跨模态对齐和双向掩码恢复模块实现检索，并构建了包含14668张图像的大规模数据集T2I-VeRW。

<h3 id="253">[253] Quantizing With Randomized Hadamard Transforms: Efficient Heuristic Now Proven</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.06014](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06014) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.DS; cs.NI
- **Tags**: Quantization, Optimization, Deep Learning Theory
- **Summary**: 本文证明了两个随机化Hadamard变换的组合在量化任务中能够渐近匹配均匀随机旋转的性能，为现代压缩方案中广泛使用的启发式方法提供了理论保证。

<h3 id="254">[254] Does Synthetic Data Help? Empirical Evidence from Deep Learning Time Series Forecasters</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.06032](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06032) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hugo Cazaux, Eyjólfur Ingi Ásgeirsson, Hlynur Stefánsson
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Time Series Forecasting, Data Synthesis, Data Augmentation
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/hugoiscracked/synthetic-ts/tree/main)
- **Summary**: 本文对合成数据在时间序列预测中的作用进行了大规模实证研究，发现通道混合模型受益而通道独立模型受损，在低资源设置下合成增强可使TimesNet超越全数据基线。

<h3 id="255">[255] When AI Meets Science: Research Diversity, Interdisciplinarity, Visibility, and Retractions across Disciplines in a Global Surge</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.06033](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06033) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Andrés F. Castro Torres, Joan Giner-Miguelez, Mercè Crosas
- **Subjects**: cs.DL; cs.AI; cs.CY; cs.SI
- **Tags**: Science Policy, AI Ethics, Research Reproducibility
- **Summary**: 本文分析了1960-2015年AI在科学领域的采用情况，发现2015年后呈指数增长，但AI支持的研究局限于少数主题，存在不当的引用溢价和更高的撤稿率，揭示了开放性和可重复性挑战。

<h3 id="256">[256] Quantum Kernels for Audio Deepfake Detection Using Spectrogram Patch Features</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.06035](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06035) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI

- **Summary**: 本文提出Q-Patch量子特征映射方法，将mel频谱图的时频块编码为量子态用于音频深度伪造检测，在低资源设置下实现了0.87的AUROC，优于经典基线的0.82。

<h3 id="257">[257] Optimal Transport for LLM Reward Modeling from Noisy Preference</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.06036](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06036) (cross-listed)

- **Summary**: 该论文提出了SelectiveRM框架，利用最优传输方法处理RLHF中存在噪声偏好的奖励建模问题。通过联合一致性差异和质量松弛机制，该框架能够自动排除与语义一致性矛盾的噪声样本。

<h3 id="258">[258] TFM-Retouche: A Lightweight Input-Space Adapter for Tabular Foundation Models</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI

- **Summary**: 该论文提出了TFM-Retouche，一种轻量级输入空间适配器，用于表格基础模型的自适应。该方法通过学习输入空间的残差校正来对齐数据与预训练模型的归纳偏置，在TabArena-Lite基准上取得了最佳性能。

<h3 id="259">[259] Causal Reinforcement Learning for Complex Card Games: A Magic The Gathering Benchmark</h3>

<h3 id="260">[260] Normalized Architectures are Natively 4-Bit</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI

- **Summary**: 该论文证明了nGPT架构（将权重和隐藏表示约束在单位超球面上）对4位精度训练具有内在的鲁棒性。超球面约束增强了信号的正相关积累，提高了有效信噪比，无需额外干预即可实现稳定的低精度训练。

<h3 id="261">[261] Milestone-Guided Policy Learning for Long-Horizon Language Agents</h3>

<h3 id="262">[262] VISD: Enhancing Video Reasoning via Structured Self-Distillation</h3>

<h3 id="263">[263] CredibleDFGO: Differentiable Factor Graph Optimization with Credibility Supervision</h3>

<h3 id="264">[264] Beyond Autoregressive RTG: Conditioning via Injection Outside Sequential Modeling in Decision Transformer</h3>

<h3 id="265">[265] Schedule-and-Calibrate: Utility-Guided Multi-Task Reinforcement Learning for Code LLMs</h3>

<h3 id="266">[266] Dynamic Pondering Sparsity-aware Mixture-of-Experts Transformer for Event Stream based Visual Object Tracking</h3>

<h3 id="267">[267] Continuous Expert Assembly: Instance-Conditioned Low-Rank Residuals for All-in-One Image Restoration</h3>

<h3 id="268">[268] BUILD-AND-FIND: An Effort-Aware Protocol for Evaluating Agent-Managed Codebases</h3>

<h3 id="269">[269] Autoregressive Visual Generation Needs a Prologue</h3>

<h3 id="270">[270] Listwise Policy Optimization: Group-based RLVR as Target-Projection on the LLM Response Simplex</h3>

<h3 id="271">[271] SymDrift: One-Shot Generative Modeling under Symmetries</h3>

<h3 id="272">[272] IRC-Bench: Recognizing Entities from Contextual Cues in First-Person Reminiscences</h3>

<h3 id="273">[273] AI-Generated Images: What Humans and Machines See When They Look at the Same Image</h3>

<h3 id="274">[274] Unifying Goal-Conditioned RL and Unsupervised Skill Learning via Control-Maximization</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Multimodal Learning, Data Synthesis
- **Summary**: 本文提出Hard Negative Captions(HNC)数据集，包含自动生成的困难负样本标题，用于图像-文本匹配训练，提升视觉语言模型的细粒度跨模态理解能力。

<h3 id="279">[279] Retina-RAG: Retrieval-Augmented Vision-Language Modeling for Joint Retinal Diagnosis and Clinical Report Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.06173](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06173) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Abdelrahman Zaian, Sheethal Bhat, Mohamed Abdalkader, Andreas Maier
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: RAG, Medical AI, Vision-Language Model
- **Summary**: 本文提出Retina-RAG框架，结合视网膜分类器、视觉语言模型和RAG模块，实现糖尿病视网膜病变严重程度分级、黄斑水肿检测和临床报告生成的联合任务。

<h3 id="280">[280] In-Context Black-Box Optimization with Unreliable Feedback</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.06187](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06187) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Nicolas Samuel Blumer, Julien Martinelli, Samuel Kaski
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Optimization, In-Context Learning, Bayesian Optimization
- **Summary**: 本文研究反馈信息的上下文黑盒优化(FICBO)，预训练的Transformer优化器同时利用优化历史和辅助反馈信号，能够有效利用有用反馈同时对弱或误导性反馈保持鲁棒。

<h3 id="281">[281] EA-WM: Event-Aware Generative World Model with Structured Kinematic-to-Visual Action Fields</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.06192](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06192) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhaoyang Yang, Yurun Jin, Lizhe Qi, Cong Huang, Kai Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.RO
- **Tags**: World Model, Robotics, Diffusion Model
- **Summary**: 本文提出EA-WM事件感知生成世界模型，将动作和运动状态投影到目标相机视图作为结构化运动到视觉动作场，在WorldArena基准上取得最先进性能。

<h3 id="282">[282] Taming the Entropy Cliff: Variable Codebook Size Quantization for Autoregressive Visual Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.06207](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06207) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Bowen Zheng, Weijian Luo, Guang Yang, Colin Zhang, Tianyang Hu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Autoregressive Model, Image Generation, Quantization
- **Summary**: 本文发现离散视觉分词器中的"熵悬崖"现象，提出可变码本大小量化(VCQ)方法，使码本大小沿序列单调增长，显著改善了自回归视觉生成质量。

<h3 id="283">[283] Super-Level-Set Regression: Conditional Quantiles via Volume Minimization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.06210](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06210) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sacha Braun, Michael I. Jordan, Francis Bach
- **Subjects**: stat.ML; cs.AI; cs.LG; stat.AP; stat.ME
- **Tags**: Uncertainty Estimation, Optimization
- **Summary**: 本文提出超水平集回归(SLS)框架，通过直接参数化和优化条件水平集的几何边界来解决多元回归中的最小体积预测区域问题，无需估计完整条件密度。

<h3 id="284">[284] Contrastive Identification and Generation in the Limit</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.06211](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06211) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xiaoyu Li, Andi Han, Jiaojiao Jiang, Junbin Gao
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.DS
- **Tags**: Deep Learning Theory, Contrastive Learning
- **Summary**: 本文开创了对比识别和生成极限的研究，学习者观察对比数据对而非正例，给出了对比可识别类的精确刻画，并证明了在有限对抗损坏下对比学习优于正例学习。

<h3 id="285">[285] FunctionalAgent: Towards end-to-end on-top functional design</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.06215](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06215) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yuhao Chen, Donald G. Truhlar, Xiao He
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Molecular Generation, Scientific Discovery
- **Summary**: 本文介绍FunctionalAgent智能体系统，用于多构型对密度泛函理论(MC-PDFT)中泛函的全自动开发，开发了MC26和COF26泛函，在训练集和测试集上取得最优性能。

<h3 id="286">[286] TIDE: Every Layer Knows the Token Beneath the Context</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.06216](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06216) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ajay Jaiswal, Lauren Hannah, Han-Byul Kim, Duc Hoang, Mehrdad Farajtabar, Minsik Cho
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Transformer Architecture, LLM Training, Memory Architecture
- **Summary**: 本文提出TIDE方法，通过在每层注入EmbeddingMemory来解决传统LLM中罕见词嵌入训练不足和上下文坍缩问题，在语言建模和下游任务中取得性能提升。

<h3 id="287">[287] When to Trust Imagination: Adaptive Action Execution for World Action Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.06222](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06222) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Rui Wang, Yue Zhang, Jiehong Lin, Kuncheng Luo, Jianan Wang, Zhongrui Wang, Xiaojuan Qi
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: World Model, Robotics, Model-Based RL
- **Summary**: 本文提出FFDC验证器，使机器人能够根据预测-观测一致性自适应调整世界动作模型的执行块大小，在RoboTwin基准和真实世界实验中实现了鲁棒性与效率的良好平衡。

<h3 id="288">[288] Memory Inception: Latent-Space KV Cache Manipulation for Steering LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.06225](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06225) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Andy Zeyi Liu, Michael Zhang, Ilana Greenberg, Adam Alnasser, Lucas Baker, John Sous
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Inference, KV Cache, Prompt Engineering
- **Summary**: 本文提出Memory Inception(MI)方法，通过在选定层插入文本派生的KV缓存来引导LLM，在个性引导、可更新指导和结构化推理任务中实现了比提示和激活引导更好的控制-漂移权衡。

<h3 id="289">[289] Soft Deterministic Policy Gradient with Gaussian Smoothing</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.06228](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06228) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hyunjun Na, Donghwan Lee
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Policy Gradient, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了一种基于高斯平滑的Soft-DPG方法，解决了传统DPG在稀疏或离散奖励下因Critic不可微而导致的梯度不稳定问题。该方法消除了对Critic动作梯度的依赖，并在连续控制基准测试中表现出色，特别是在离散奖励环境下优于标准DDPG。

<h3 id="290">[290] OBLIQ-Bench: Exposing Overlooked Bottlenecks in Modern Retrievers with Latent and Implicit Queries</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.06235](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06235) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Diane Tchuindjo, Devavrat Shah, Omar Khattab
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Information Retrieval, Benchmark, LLM Evaluation
- **Summary**: 该论文定义了一类寻找潜在模式或隐含立场的“倾斜”查询，并提出了OBLIQ-Bench基准来评估检索系统处理此类查询的能力。研究发现，虽然LLM能够很好地识别相关性，但现有的检索管道在召回相关文档方面存在显著不足。

<h3 id="291">[291] Band Together: Untargeted Adversarial Training with Multimodal Coordination against Evasion-based Promotion Attacks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.06238](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06238) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Guanmeng Xian, Ning Yang, Philip S. Yu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Recommender System, Adversarial Robustness, Multimodal Learning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/gmXian/UAT-MC)
- **Summary**: 该论文针对多模态推荐系统中的逃避式推广攻击，提出了一种名为UAT-MC的多模态协调对抗训练方法。该方法解决了跨模态梯度不匹配的问题，显著提高了模型对推广攻击的鲁棒性，同时保持了推荐性能。

<h3 id="292">[292] Cumulative-Goodness Free-Riding in Forward-Forward Networks: Real, Repairable, but Not Accuracy-Dominant</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.06240](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06240) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Amirhossein Yousefiramandi
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Deep Learning Theory, Optimization
- **Summary**: 该论文研究了Forward-Forward网络训练中存在的“层搭便车”现象，即后续层利用前层已分离的任务。作者提出了三种局部修正方法来改善层分离统计量，但发现在非退化训练过程中，该现象并非限制最终准确率的主导因素。

<h3 id="293">[293] The Weight Gram Matrix Captures Sequential Feature Linearization in Deep Networks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.06258](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06258) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Taehun Cha, Daniel Beaglehole, Adityanarayanan Radhakrishnan, Donghun Lee
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Deep Learning Theory, Representation Learning
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了一个以特征为中心的分析框架，通过引入特征学习方程，证明了权重Gram矩阵是捕捉特征动力学的关键对象。研究发现深度网络通过学习将表示逐步转化为目标线性结构，为神经崩溃等现象提供了统一的解释。

<h3 id="294">[294] Inference-Time Refinement Closes the Synthetic-Real Gap in Tabular Diffusion</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.06261](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06261) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Eugenio Lomurno, Filippo Balzarini, Francesco Benelle, Francesca Pia Panaccione, Matteo Matteucci
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Diffusion Model, Tabular Learning, Data Synthesis
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了TARDIS框架，通过在推理时对预训练的表格扩散模型进行细化，成功缩小了合成数据与真实数据之间的效用差距。该方法利用双向倒角细化模式，在多个基准测试中实现了优于真实数据的下游任务表现。

<h3 id="295">[295] Linear Semantic Segmentation for Low-Resource Spoken Dialects</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.06276](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06276) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Kirill Chirkunov, Younes Samih, Abed Alhakim Freihat, Hanan Aldarmaki
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Discourse Segmentation, Low-Resource NLP
- **Venue**: ACL 2026
- **Summary**: 该论文针对低资源口语方言（如方言阿拉伯语）的语义分割问题，构建了一个包含多种体裁的新基准，并提出了一种针对局部语义连贯性的分割模型。实验表明，该方法在方言非新闻体裁上显著优于强基线，并可推广至其他低资源口语。

<h3 id="296">[296] Correct Code, Vulnerable Dependencies: A Large Scale Measurement Study of LLM-Specified Library Versions</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.06279](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06279) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Chengjie Wang, Jingzheng Wu, Xiang Ling, Tianyue Luo, Chen Zhao
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Code Generation, LLM Security, Software Engineering
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/dw763j/PinTrace)
- **Summary**: 该论文对LLM生成代码中的第三方库版本选择进行了大规模测量研究，发现LLM倾向于指定存在已知安全漏洞（CVE）和兼容性问题的版本。研究揭示了LLM版本选择存在系统性偏差，构成了一个此前被忽视的风险面。

<h3 id="297">[297] A Topological Sorting Criterion for Random Causal Directed Acyclic Graphs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.06288](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06288) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Alexander G. Reisach, Antoine Chambaz, Gilles Blanchard, Sebastian Weichwald
- **Subjects**: stat.ME; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Causal Discovery, Graph Learning
- **Summary**: 该论文证明了在随机有向无环图（DAG）中，通过开放路径可达的节点集（亲属集）沿因果顺序单调递增。基于此性质，作者提出了一种通过估计亲属数量来恢复因果顺序的拓扑排序准则，并讨论了其对因果发现算法评估的影响。

<h3 id="298">[298] Multimodal Deep Generative Model for Semi-Supervised Learning under Class Imbalance</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.06289](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06289) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Heegeon Yoon, Heeyoung Kim
- **Subjects**: stat.ML; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Semi-Supervised Learning, Multimodal Learning, Long-Tailed Learning
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了一种针对类别不平衡数据的多模态深度生成模型，用于半监督学习。该方法利用Student's t分布捕捉重尾潜在分布，并通过乘积专家方法融合多模态信息，在基准和真实数据集上取得了优越的分类性能。

<h3 id="299">[299] Log-Likelihood, Simpson's Paradox, and the Detection of Machine-Generated Text</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.06294](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06294) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Tom Kempton, Viktor Drobnyi, Maeve Madigan, Stuart Burrell
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: AI-Generated Text Detection, LLM Evaluation
- **Summary**: 该论文指出在机器生成文本检测中，简单平均似然分数会导致辛普森悖论，破坏局部信号。作者提出了一种基于贝叶斯决策理论的学习局部校准步骤，通过聚合校准后的对数似然比，显著提升了检测性能。

<h3 id="300">[300] Attributions All the Way Down? The Metagame of Interpretability</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.06295](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06295) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hubert Baniecki, Przemyslaw Biecek, Fabian Fumagalli
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Interpretability, Explainable AI
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了“元博弈”框架，用于量化模型解释的二阶交互效应，即特征对归因的影响。作者证明了归因可以分层分解为元归因，并在指令微调语言模型、视觉语言编码器和多模态扩散模型中展示了其应用价值。

<h3 id="301">[301] Render, Don't Decode: Weight-Space World Models with Latent Structural Disentanglement</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.06298](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06298) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Roussel Desmond Nzoyem, Mauro Comi
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: World Model, Neural Rendering, Video Generation
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了NOVA世界建模框架，将系统状态表示为隐式神经表示（INR）的权重，从而消除了对重型解码器的依赖。该方法不仅提高了计算效率和可解释性，还能在无监督情况下解耦场景的结构组件，实现可控预测。

<h3 id="302">[302] Pro-KLShampoo: Projected KL-Shampoo with Whitening Recovered by Orthogonalization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.06316](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06316) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ruotong Sun, Ermin Wei
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Optimization, LLM Training
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了一种名为Pro-KLShampoo的优化器，结合了KL-Shampoo的Kronecker分解预调节和梯度正交化技术。该方法利用特征值的尖峰-平坦结构，在LLM预训练中显著提升了验证损失、内存占用和训练时间等指标。

<h3 id="303">[303] NavOne: One-Step Global Planning for Vision-Language Navigation on Top-Down Maps</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.06317](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06317) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Dijia Zhan, Jinyi Li, Chenxi Zheng, Shaoyu Huang, Yong Li, Jie Tang, Xuemiao Xu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Navigation, Embodied AI
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了一种名为NavOne的框架，将视觉语言导航（VLN）重构为基于俯视图的单步全局路径规划问题。该方法通过直接预测路径概率，在R2R-TopDown数据集上实现了最先进的性能，并大幅提升了规划效率。

<h3 id="304">[304] Improving the Efficiency of Language Agent Teams with Adaptive Task Graphs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.06320](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06320) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Elizabeth Mieczkowski, Alexander Ku, Tiwalayo Eisape, Dilip Arumugam, John Matters, Katherine M. Collins, Ilia Sucholutsky, Thomas L. Griffiths
- **Subjects**: cs.MA; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Multi-Agent System, Workflow Automation
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了LATTE框架，通过构建和维护共享的自适应任务图来协调LLM团队，解决了现有方法效率低下的问题。实验表明，该方法在减少资源消耗和协调失败的同时，保持了与现有设计相当或更高的准确性。

<h3 id="305">[305] Measuring Evaluation-Context Divergence in Open-Weight LLMs: A Paired-Prompt Protocol with Pilot Evidence of Alignment-Pipeline-Specific Heterogeneity</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.06327](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06327) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Florian A. D. Burnat, Brittany I. Davidson
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, LLM Alignment
- **Summary**: 本文提出了一种配对提示协议来测量大语言模型在评估语境与部署语境下的行为差异，发现不同模型表现出显著的异质性：部分模型在评估语境下更加谨慎，而其他模型则在部署语境下更加谨慎。

<h3 id="306">[306] Fine-Tuning Small Language Models for Solution-Oriented Windows Event Log Analysis</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.06330](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06330) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Siraaj Akhtar, Saad Khan, Simon Parkinson
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Small Language Model, Log Analysis, Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning
- **Summary**: 本文研究了小型语言模型在Windows事件日志分析中的应用，通过创建大规模合成数据集并使用LoRA微调技术，证明微调后的SLM在问题识别和解决方案生成方面优于大型语言模型。

<h3 id="307">[307] TinyBayes: Closed-Form Bayesian Inference via Jacobi Prior for Real-Time Image Classification on Edge Devices</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.06333](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06333) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shouvik Sardar, Sourish Das
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG; stat.AP; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Edge Computing, Image Classification, Agricultural AI
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/shouvik-sardar/TinyBayes)
- **Summary**: 本文提出了TinyBayes框架，将闭式贝叶斯分类器与移动级计算机视觉管道相结合，用于边缘设备上的作物病害检测，实现了低于9.5MB的模型大小和150ms以内的推理时间。

<h3 id="308">[308] CoupleEvo: Evolving Heuristics for Coupled Optimization Problems Using Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.06341](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06341) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Thomas Bömer, Bastian Amberg, Max Disselnmeyer, Anne Meyer
- **Subjects**: cs.NE; cs.AI; math.OC
- **Tags**: Evolutionary Computation, Optimization, LLM Reasoning
- **Venue**: GECCO 2026 Workshop
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/tb-git-kit-research/CoupleEvo)
- **Summary**: 本文提出了CoupleEvo方法，利用大语言模型为耦合优化问题演化启发式算法，提出了三种演化协调策略并在实验中验证了分解策略的有效性。

<h3 id="309">[309] Human-AI Co-Evolution and Epistemic Collapse: A Dynamical Systems Perspective</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.06347](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06347) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xuening Wu, Yanlan Kang, Qianya Xu, Kexuan Xie, Jiaqi Mi, Honggang Wang, Yubin Liu, Zeping Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.HC; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Model Collapse, Deep Learning Theory, Human-AI Collaboration
- **Summary**: 本文从动力系统角度提出人类与语言模型形成耦合动力系统的统一视角，识别出三种动力学区域：协同进化增强、脆弱平衡和退化收敛，揭示了过度依赖AI可能导致信息瓶颈。

<h3 id="310">[310] Is Escalation Worth It? A Decision-Theoretic Characterization of LLM Cascades</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.06350](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06350) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Dylan Bouchard
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Inference, Decision Making
- **Summary**: 本文建立了基于决策理论的LLM级联框架，分析了成本-质量前沿的几何特性，发现级联性能主要受结构成本限制而非中间阶段不足的影响。

<h3 id="311">[311] Topological Signatures of Grokking</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.06352](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06352) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yifan Tang, Qiquan Wang, Inés García-Redondo, Anthea Monod
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Grokking, Representation Learning, Deep Learning Theory
- **Summary**: 本文通过持久同调研究神经网络的grokking现象，发现一阶同调的最大持久性和总持久性急剧增加是grokking的清晰拓扑特征，揭示了神经网络如何内化任务的潜在结构。

<h3 id="312">[312] Memory Efficient Full-gradient Attacks (MEFA) Framework for Adversarial Defense Evaluations</h3>

<h3 id="320">[320] E = T*H/(O+B): A Dimensionless Control Parameter for Mixture-of-Experts Ecology</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.CV

<h3 id="321">[321] COVID-19 Infodemic. Understanding content features in detecting fake news using a machine learning approach</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.06435](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06435) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Balakrishnan Vimala, Hii Lee Zing, Laporte Eric

<h3 id="322">[322] Constraint Decay: The Fragility of LLM Agents in Backend Code Generation</h3>

<h3 id="324">[324] Q-MMR: Off-Policy Evaluation via Recursive Reweighting and Moment Matching</h3>

<h3 id="380">[380] Making AI Evaluation Deployment Relevant Through Context Specification</h3>

<h3 id="451">[451] FIT to Forget: Robust Continual Unlearning for Large Language Models</h3>

<h3 id="453">[453] Keep Rehearsing and Refining: Lifelong Learning Vehicle Routing under Continually Drifting Tasks</h3>

<h3 id="454">[454] DialectLLM: A Dialect-Aware Dialog[ue] Generation Framework Beyond Standard American English</h3>

<h3 id="456">[456] The Illusion of Forgetting: Attack Unlearned Diffusion via Initial Latent Variable Optimization</h3>

<h3 id="469">[469] Visual Para-Thinker: Divide-and-Conquer Reasoning for Visual Comprehension</h3>

<h3 id="470">[470] On the Rate-Distortion-Complexity Tradeoff for Semantic Communication</h3>

<h3 id="471">[471] Molecular Design beyond Training Data with Novel Extended Objective Functionals of Generative AI Models Driven by Quantum Annealing Computer</h3>

<h3 id="472">[472] Perceptive Humanoid Parkour: Chaining Dynamic Human Skills via Motion Matching</h3>

<h3 id="473">[473] Decentralized Attention Fails Centralized Signals: Rethinking Transformers for Medical Time Series</h3>

<h3 id="474">[474] CAMEL: Confidence-Gated Reflection for Reward Modeling</h3>

<h3 id="475">[475] Same Words, Different Judgments: How Preferences Vary Across Modalities</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI; cs.HC

<h3 id="476">[476] AI Agents Alone Are Not (Yet) Sufficient for Social Simulation</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.MA; cs.AI; cs.CE; cs.CY; cs.SI
